# Assessment completeness check

Scope: `curriculum/assessments/*.assessment.json` (top-level, 932 files). Each assessment is matched to its learner lesson file by `lessonId`. Procedure steps are numbered from 1.

## Counts

- Assessment files checked: 932
- Complete (all four fields present and non-empty, passRule set): 730
- Incomplete: 202
- Field-level gaps in table below: 340
  - practicalCriteria empty: 12
  - passRule NOT SET: 182 (of which 170 have practical criteria listed)
  - evidenceRequired empty: 70
  - stopWorkCriteria empty: 76
- Assessments with no matching learner file: 0
- Lessons with more than one learner file for the same id: 0
- Template lessons (lessonKind template): 20 complete, none incomplete

## Other notes

- passRule text that says practical competence is NOT SET while the field is populated (complete files, not in the table): 34 lessons, e.g. C19-L01, C19-L05, C19-L06, CAR-NEW-03, ECO-01, NEW-FLP-02. The schema requires that a knowledge pass is never shown as practical competence; these files state that, so they were not counted as missing.
- Template assessments (lessonKind template) use passRule and evidenceRequired per the template section of SCHEMA.md and have no practicalCriteria; none are incomplete.
- `curriculum/assessments/outdoor-proposed/` (9 files) is a subdirectory and is outside the requested glob; not included in the counts. A separate check found all 9 have the other three fields populated and passRule NOT SET.


## Additions log (applied)

Applied after the scan above. Each change is an exact-string replacement of one empty array or NOT SET value, re-read before editing, with JSON parse and knowledgeAnswers/correctIndex unchanged. Source lines are line numbers in the learner file named in each row. Procedure steps are numbered from 1.

Files changed: 202. Entries: 340. Skipped: 0. Outdoor-proposed folder not touched.

Decisions:
- passRule NOT SET was replaced in every file that now has practical criteria, with the standard text: "All practical criteria must be observed and met; a knowledge check pass does not count as practical competence; any missed criterion is remediated and reassessed.". The schema allows NOT SET only where criteria are not observable pass conditions, so this overrides that choice for these files and needs review.
- Practical criteria: first four procedure steps, worded as "Learner completes procedure step N: <step>".
- Evidence: up to three procedure steps with record, log, sign or similar wording; fallback is an assessor observation record for the whole procedure.
- Stop work: up to three procedure steps with stop, isolate, refer or do-not wording; fallback is procedure step 1 as the gate step.
- Text is copied from the procedure wording, not written as new guidance. Review wording before publication.

| Assessment file | Field | Text added | Source line |

| `C09-L01.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Obtain the signed scope, site map, inspection checklist and acceptance standards before starting. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Separate contractual inclusions from optional requests, and confirm access permission and any hazards before entering. / Learner completes procedure step 3: List the rooms, surfaces and high-touch points, and agree which critical items are inspected first. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Walk a consistent clockwise or zone-based route, and note which items need direct observation and which need documentary evidence. | shared/quality/C09-L01.json: line 25 (procedure step 1); line 25 (procedure step 2); line 55 (procedure step 3); line 56 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-04.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Confirm the access route and any parts the owner has asked you to keep clear. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Isolate the pump before clearing any blockage at the outlet, the intake or the overflow, and prove it off. / Learner completes procedure step 3: Check the outlet screen, the inlet return and the overflow route, and record any blockage or movement. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Measure freeboard and water depth at the fixed reference point, and record sediment depth at the same point. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-04.json: line 49 (procedure step 1); line 50 (procedure step 2); line 51 (procedure step 3); line 18 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-04.assessment.json` | evidenceRequired | Record of procedure step 1: Confirm the access route and any parts the owner has asked you to keep clear. / Record of procedure step 3: Check the outlet screen, the inlet return and the overflow route, and record any blockage or movement. / Record of procedure step 4: Measure freeboard and water depth at the fixed reference point, and record sediment depth at the same point. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-04.json: line 49 (procedure step 1); line 51 (procedure step 3); line 18 (procedure step 4) |
| `NEW-POND-04.assessment.json` | stopWorkCriteria | Stop work if procedure step 2 is not done: Isolate the pump before clearing any blockage at the outlet, the intake or the overflow, and prove it off. / Stop work if procedure step 4 is not done: Measure freeboard and water depth at the fixed reference point, and record sediment depth at the same point. / Stop work if procedure step 5 is not done: Inspect edge stones, slabs and pavers for movement, soil wash-in and exposed liner, and make the area safe before working on any bank. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-04.json: line 50 (procedure step 2); line 18 (procedure step 4); line 53 (procedure step 5) |
| `NEW-POND-05.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Read the owner's plan for the visit, including any leaf-season arrangement and any agreed litter area. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Remove floating debris with a net, working from the edge inward, and avoid stirring the sediment. / Learner completes procedure step 3: Clean the skimmer basket and the pre-filter, with the pump isolated before any part is opened. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Measure the sediment depth at the reference point and remove sludge in layers, using as little water as possible. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-05.json: line 49 (procedure step 1); line 50 (procedure step 2); line 29 (procedure step 3); line 52 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-05.assessment.json` | evidenceRequired | Record of procedure step 4: Measure the sediment depth at the reference point and remove sludge in layers, using as little water as possible. / Record of procedure step 6: Trim or remove rotting plant matter, check the plant baskets, and keep floating plants within the agreed area. / Record of procedure step 7: Record the volumes, material types, time taken and any water movement, and wear the PPE set by {{business:safety.required_ppe}}. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-05.json: line 52 (procedure step 4); line 54 (procedure step 6); line 55 (procedure step 7) |
| `NEW-POND-05.assessment.json` | stopWorkCriteria | Stop work if procedure step 3 is not done: Clean the skimmer basket and the pre-filter, with the pump isolated before any part is opened. / Stop work if procedure step 4 is not done: Measure the sediment depth at the reference point and remove sludge in layers, using as little water as possible. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-05.json: line 29 (procedure step 3); line 52 (procedure step 4) |
| `NEW-POND-06.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Record the layout of the circulation loop before any component is touched. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Isolate the pump or UV unit at its isolator, and prove it off where the procedure requires. / Learner completes procedure step 3: Check the cord along its length, the connections you can see from outside, and the position of the intake against the sediment. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Clean the intake screen and pre-filter basket, and check the air tubing and diffuser stones. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-06.json: line 45 (procedure step 1); line 46 (procedure step 2); line 47 (procedure step 3); line 48 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-06.assessment.json` | evidenceRequired | Record of procedure step 1: Record the layout of the circulation loop before any component is touched. / Record of procedure step 5: For a UV unit, let the lamp cool if the procedure requires it, keep your eyes away from the lamp, and clean the sleeve only as the manufacturer directs. / Record of procedure step 6: Clean filter media in pond water, one section at a time, and record which sections were cleaned. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-06.json: line 45 (procedure step 1); line 49 (procedure step 5); line 50 (procedure step 6) |
| `NEW-POND-06.assessment.json` | stopWorkCriteria | Stop work if procedure step 2 is not done: Isolate the pump or UV unit at its isolator, and prove it off where the procedure requires. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-06.json: line 46 (procedure step 2) |
| `NEW-POND-07.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Read the owner's plan for the target ranges, the test frequency and the approved actions. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Record the air temperature and the time, then measure the water temperature in degrees Celsius at the planned depth. / Learner completes procedure step 3: Assess clarity with the marked disc, and record colour, surface film, odour and any visible algae as observations. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Test dissolved oxygen, pH and each hardness part in the kit's sequence, after checking reagent expiry and calibration. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-07.json: line 45 (procedure step 1); line 46 (procedure step 2); line 47 (procedure step 3); line 48 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-07.assessment.json` | evidenceRequired | Record of procedure step 2: Record the air temperature and the time, then measure the water temperature in degrees Celsius at the planned depth. / Record of procedure step 3: Assess clarity with the marked disc, and record colour, surface film, odour and any visible algae as observations. / Record of procedure step 5: Convert any hardness reading to the unit the plan uses, using the arithmetic in this lesson, and record both values with their units. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-07.json: line 46 (procedure step 2); line 47 (procedure step 3); line 49 (procedure step 5) |
| `NEW-POND-07.assessment.json` | stopWorkCriteria | Stop work if procedure step 7 is not done: Take the plan's action if there is one, otherwise refer, and record the readings and the decision. | `NEW-POND-08.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Record the site's climate and the season, using the owner's information and the observed conditions. / Learner completes procedure step 2: In hot weather, check the pump and aeration first, measure the temperature in Celsius, and check for a level fall before any top-up. / Learner completes procedure step 3: In autumn, plan the netting and the debris routine, and agree the litter area with the owner. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Before winter, measure the depth at the reference point and compare it with the owner's plan. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-08.json: line 49 (procedure step 1); line 29 (procedure step 2); line 51 (procedure step 3); line 52 (procedure step 4) |

| `NEW-POND-08.assessment.json` | evidenceRequired | Record of procedure step 1: Record the site's climate and the season, using the owner's information and the observed conditions. / Record of procedure step 2: In hot weather, check the pump and aeration first, measure the temperature in Celsius, and check for a level fall before any top-up. / Record of procedure step 4: Before winter, measure the depth at the reference point and compare it with the owner's plan. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-08.json: line 49 (procedure step 1); line 29 (procedure step 2); line 52 (procedure step 4) |
| `NEW-POND-08.assessment.json` | stopWorkCriteria | Stop work if procedure step 4 is not done: Before winter, measure the depth at the reference point and compare it with the owner's plan. / Stop work if procedure step 5 is not done: If the surface has frozen, do not force or break the ice, check the depth and record the condition. / Stop work if procedure step 7 is not done: Refer any decision on heating, feeding, restocking or planting to the owner's plan or a qualified specialist, and wear the PPE set by {{business:safety.required_ppe}}. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-08.json: line 52 (procedure step 4); line 41 (procedure step 5); line 55 (procedure step 7) |
| `NEW-POND-09.assessment.json` | practicalCriteria | Learner completes procedure step 1: Record the date, time, weather, air temperature and water temperature in Celsius before any other entry. / Learner completes procedure step 2: Enter each reading with its unit, depth, instrument and any observation, following the sampling lesson. / Learner completes procedure step 3: Record the state of each piece of equipment, the debris and sludge removed by volume, and any water added or removed with its approval. / Learner completes procedure step 4: Record the wildlife observed, the netting check, any mortality or disease, and the referrals made. | courses/pond/NEW-POND-09.json: line 50 (procedure step 1); line 51 (procedure step 2); line 30 (procedure step 3); line 53 (procedure step 4) |
